- Item photos, App (2026-10-17, spec 4.4, 4.6, 7). Local: `list_items.photo_path` in src/db/schema.ts and migration 0023_item_photo_path (drizzle-kit generate); pull and the outbox are generic over the schema, so it syncs and merges per field with no sync code changes. **Shared photo code**: recipe-photo-source.ts now has `ownPhotoSource(bucket, path, session)` (authenticated URL, path as `cacheKey`, bearer header) and `usePhotoFallback(key, own)` (one retry with a refreshed token on 400/401/403, then fallback); `recipePhotoSource`/`useRecipePhoto` use them, behaviour unchanged. photo-upload.ts: new `pickPhoto(from)` returns the photo full size; `pickRecipePhoto` is pickPhoto + downscale, as before. **New src/features/lists/item-photos.ts**: `ITEM_PHOTOS_BUCKET`, `itemPhotoPath`, `itemPhotoSource`, `useItemPhoto`, `uploadItemPhoto(itemId, localUri)` (downscale `UPLOAD_PHOTO`, upload to `item-photos` at a new path, then `setItemPhoto`, then the change for "Photo updated"), `retryItemPhotoUpload`, `removeItemPhoto(itemId)`, `undoItemPhotoChange`, upload/change stores like the recipe's. items.ts: `setItemPhoto`, `getItemPhoto`, `itemPhotoQuery`, and `photoPath` in `listItemsQuery`/`ItemView` (optional on the type). **New item-photo.tsx** (`ItemPhotoSection`, under the title block, above the Amount card): no photo → dashed 64 pt Add photo tile, an @expo/ui `Menu` of Take photo / Choose from library; a photo → `Card` 8 pt in, 4:3, radius md, with an @expo/ui `ContextMenu` (long-press) and a round … `Menu`, both Replace photo (submenu of the two sources) and Remove photo (destructive); uploading → the local image with a spinner and no menus; a photo that won't load → `photo` glyph placeholder, menus kept. Tap → full-screen viewer: a `Modal` with a native zooming ScrollView (pinch to 4×; an unzoomed pull down of 80 pt closes, `viewerDragCloses`), a close button, VoiceOver escape. I used the ScrollView's zoom rather than gesture-handler/reanimated: it's native and has fewer moving parts. Toasts ("Photo updated"/"Photo removed" + Undo, "Couldn't upload the photo" + Try again) render inline right under the photo rather than at the sheet's bottom, because the item sheet is a formSheet with detents and a bottom-pinned sibling of its ScrollView risks the iOS 26 zero-height bug noted in themed-view.tsx. **Offline** (new shared hook src/hooks/use-online.ts, moved out of settings/notifications.tsx, which now imports it): the tile is disabled with "Needs a connection"; Replace photo is a disabled menu item with "Needs a connection" as its subtitle, and VoiceOver's replace actions go; Remove still works offline (it's a local, synced write). VoiceOver: "Photo of za'atar" (the stored name), hint "Opens it full screen", custom actions Take photo / Choose from library / Remove photo. **Row**: a `photo` glyph (iconSmall, inkSecondary) after the name, before any NEW badge; the label gets "has a photo" (`itemRowLabel` takes a `photo` part). A reopen by a new add keeps the row, so it keeps the photo. `clearPhotoCache` clears all of expo-image's caches, so it already covers item photos (comment updated). Tokens: `sizes.itemPhotoTile` 64, `sizes.itemPhotoMore` 32, `PhotoViewer.background` (#000000, `useTheme().photoViewer`); DESIGN.md has them and an "Item photo" pattern. Strings: `item.photo.*`, `lists.row.hasPhoto` in en/es. Spec: an item-photo bullet in 4.4, a row note in 4.6, a sync note in 7.1 (5 was done by the server task). Not handled: moving an item onto a list where it Combines or reopens another item drops the moved item's photo (the target keeps its own); the task didn't cover Move. Tests: new src/db/__tests__/item-photo-migration.test.ts (4: tag, no column before, existing item gets null, nullable text takes a path and a null); new lists/__tests__/item-photos.test.ts (20: path shape; source URL/cacheKey/header, signed out, none; pickPhoto full size, camera permission, cancel; upload shrinks, bucket, path, photo_path + outbox, change; Replace + Undo to the old path; failure + Try again; unreadable photo; toast keys; item deleted before/while uploading; Remove + Undo; nothing to remove; setItemPhoto writes only changes and refuses a deleted item; groupItems carries photoPath; reopen keeps it); new lists/__tests__/item-photo-sheet.test.tsx (22: the tile and its place, its menu's two items, library upload with spinner then the stored photo + toast, Undo, camera denied then allowed, cancel, failure + Try again, 4:3 card + VoiceOver label/hint/actions, both menus' items, Replace + Undo, Remove + Undo, VoiceOver remove and library actions, the full-screen viewer (zoom props, label, close, short vs long pull), 401 retry then fallback, a photo that won't load, offline tile and coming back online, offline Replace disabled + Remove works, reopen keeps it, a pulled photo appears, Spanish, `viewerDragCloses`); item-row.test.tsx +3 (glyph + label, glyph before NEW, none/null/Spanish); pull.test.ts +4 (path applied, newer remote clear, local unpushed photo beats an older remote one, a server without the column). Existing tests changed because this task changes what they check: write-paths.test.ts (a new item's outbox insert now has `photo_path: null`); item-screen.test.tsx (its mocks gained `Button`/`ContextMenu`/`disabled`, a reanimated mock for the Toast and an expo-network mock, and the Spanish unit-menu test now picks the unit menu out of the sheet's two native menus). yarn jest (4835 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. - Import closes right away (2026-10-17, spec 4.10, 4.11). src/app/import-recipe.tsx: Save first checks what fails at once and shows it inline with the sheet open: "That doesn't look like a link" (as before) and "You're offline" (new, from `useOnline`/expo-network; an invalid link wins over offline). Otherwise it starts the import, calls `router.back()` and posts the new `importingNotification(id)` (src/features/recipes/import-notifications.ts: `recipeImport`, `fork.knife`, `recipeHue(id)`, "Importing your recipe" / "We'll let you know when it's ready.", no onPress). The id comes from the new `importNotificationId()` (`recipe-import-<uuidv7>`, one per import). There's no recipe id yet, so the Importing tile's hue comes from that id, not the recipe's. The result always takes the old closed-while-reading path, posted with that same id so it replaces the Importing banner (or queues after whatever is showing): "Recipe added" (tap → the list and its toast with Undo), "Recipe saved" (tap → the recipe; this now applies from a list's menu too, where the toast used to show on that list), or "Couldn't import recipe" (tap → the sheet with the same choices, or Add recipe for photos). The device fallback runs inside importRecipe, so it still comes before a link failure. A ref stops a second Save before the sheet goes. Removed: the `open` ref, the `working` state and every disabled/editable it drove, the "Reading recipe…" label, the "You can close this…" line, the inline branch for server answers, and `recipes.import.reading` / `recipes.import.canClose` in en.ts and es.ts (nothing else used them; docs/i18n/es-review.csv is a generated export and still lists them until it's regenerated). New strings `notifications.importing` / `importingBody` (en, es). The builders keep their own default ids (failure by link, 'recipe-import-failed-photo'), and the sheet overrides them. Spec: in 4.10, "From inside the app" (a Save bullet replaces the reading bullet and the switch-off toast bullet), "From a photo", the in-app failure bullet, and "When the sheet was closed while reading", renamed "When the import finishes"; also 4.11's kinds line. Tests: in recipe-import-screens.test.tsx the expo-network mock now has `getNetworkStateAsync`, driven by `mockConnected`. Existing tests changed because the behaviour they check changed: the "while reading…" test is now "Save closes the sheet at once and posts Importing" (exact notification, no onPress, no reading state, a second submit does nothing); success → "Recipe added" with the same id, and on tap the list, toast and Undo; from a list's menu, success is the notification too; both switch-off tests now expect "Recipe saved" (tap opens the recipe) instead of a toast; the inline-failure test.each is now "a server failure replaces Importing with its reason" (plus blocked and rate_limited); the closed-while-reading success and recipe-only tests are folded into those; the failure-reopens tests read the second notify call. All the photo tests changed the same way (Importing, then Recipe added/saved; the failure wording with the same id; tap opens Add recipe). New: each import gets its own id; offline stays inline and posts nothing (link and photo), and typing clears it; an invalid link while offline says it isn't a link; the invalid-link test also checks there's no back or notify. import-notifications.test.ts +3: importingNotification's exact shape, Spanish, and importNotificationId's format and uniqueness. yarn jest (4842 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. Nothing in supabase/ changed.

- Store a recipe's original pages on the server (2026-10-17, spec 5, 6.10): new migration `20261107120000_recipe_pages.sql` adds `recipes.source_pages jsonb` with `recipes_source_pages_shape` through `private.recipe_source_pages_ok(pages, household_id, id)` (an array of 1–3 strings, each `<household_id>/<recipe_id>/<uuid>.jpg` in lowercase with the row's own household and id; I also refuse the same path twice). No `apply_mutations` or pull change was needed: both are generic over recipes columns (as the photo_path and steps migrations note), and the local column is the next task. Private bucket `recipe-pages` (jpeg, 2 MB, also in `config.toml`) with member select/insert/delete policies and no update, as `item-photos`. Queue `private.recipe_page_deletions`, filled by triggers: each old path no longer in the array (in page order) when `source_pages` changes or is cleared, the recipe's `<household>/<recipe>/` prefix when its row is deleted, and the `<household>/` prefix when a household is deleted. `claim_recipe_page_deletions` / `complete_recipe_page_deletions` are service role only, take entries at least an hour old, and drop paths a recipe points at again. A prefix also leaves out any object a recipe still points at. `request_recipe_photo_cleanup` now checks all three queues. photo-cleanup: the bucket list moved from index.ts into handler.ts as `CLEANUP_BUCKETS` with `bucketStores(client)` so it can be tested, and recipe-pages is third. Spec 5 (the recipes table, the shape note, the bucket and its cleanup, the item-photos "all three buckets" line) and 6.10 Privacy are updated. Tests: new pgTAP `45_recipe_pages.test.sql` (48: bucket, policies, shape check for too many/empty/not-an-array/non-string/wrong household/wrong recipe/not .jpg/uppercase/duplicate, apply_mutations applied/rejected/merged per field, each trigger queues the right paths, claim skips re-referenced and never-uploaded paths, recipe and household prefixes expand, cron call with only pages queued). Deno photo-cleanup +4 (the bucket list; queued recipe-pages objects are removed from recipe-pages and completed; each bucket removes only its own; a failed pages claim throws and completes nothing there). Changed an existing test: `44_item_photos.test.sql`'s last check ("with both queues empty") now also empties the recipe-pages queue, because deleting a household now queues its pages prefix too. supabase db reset and test db pass (45 files, 1309 tests); deno test passes (16); yarn jest (5010 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. I couldn't run `deno check` on photo-cleanup/index.ts (permission prompt), so the `withSupabase` admin client is checked against `CleanupClient` only by structure. No real AI calls.
- Keep the pages when a photo import succeeds (2026-10-18, spec 4.10): `recipes.sourcePages` (JSON text) in `src/db/schema.ts` and a drizzle-generated local migration, `0024_recipe_source_pages` (the task cites `0021_recipe_photo_path` as the pattern; 0024 is the next number). Sync needed no change because it's generic over columns. `photo-upload.ts`: the upload moved into a shared `uploadJpeg(bucket, path, uri)`, used by `runUpload` and by the new `uploadRecipePages(householdId, recipeId, uris)`. That uploads to `recipe-pages` (`RECIPE_PAGES_BUCKET` in recipe-photo-source.ts) one page at a time, in order, at new paths. On a failure it removes the ones that went up, warns and returns null (a failed removal only warns). `importRecipeFromPhotos` awaits it after the save, before returning (so before the result notification). It sets the pages through a new `updateRecipeSourcePages` (skipped if the recipe is gone), then deletes the page files. A failed import uploads and deletes nothing. Sheet: a `doc.text` glyph (`inkSecondary`, `iconSmall`) and the "pages are kept" `footnote` line under the thumbnails (`recipes.import.pagesKept`, en and es). Spec updated: 4.10 (the sheet note and the kept pages; "held only for the request" is gone), the recipes shape note in 5 (the local column) and a sync bullet in 7.1. The fake Supabase storage got `remove` (`storageRemovals`, `storageRemoveMock`, `storageUploadMock`). Tests: import.test.ts +5. (1) Every page is uploaded in order with its bytes and options, `source_pages` is set and pushed, and the files are deleted. (2) A second-page failure removes the first page, leaves `source_pages` null, and the recipe is saved. (3) A first-page failure removes nothing. (4) A failed import uploads nothing and keeps the files. (5) A link import uploads nothing. photo-upload.test.ts +4 for `uploadRecipePages`. New `src/db/__tests__/recipe-pages-migration.test.ts` (4). pull.test.ts +2: pages are pulled as an array and a newer clear wins; a local unpushed change beats an older remote one, field by field. recipe-import-screens +1: the note and glyph show, and the link sheet has no note. Changed existing tests: in mutate.test.ts and write-paths.test.ts, the expected recipe insert fields now include `source_pages: null`, since every insert sends the new column. yarn jest (5026 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. Nothing in supabase/ changed. No real AI calls.
